First, AI agents use Vortex to automatically generate and refine diverse algorithms, the best reaching up to $3.46\times$ higher throughput than full attention while preserving accuracy. Second, Vortex extends sparse attention to emerging architectures and very large models that are otherwise hard to experiment with, reaching up to $4.7\times$ higher throughput on the MLA-based GLM-4.7-Flash and $1.37\times$ on the 229B-parameter MiniMax-M2.7 on NVIDIA B200 GPUs.

</details>


### [334] [Benchmark Everything Everywhere All at Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06462)
*Shiyun Xiong,Dongming Wu,Peiwen Sun,Yuang Ai,Bokang Yang,Wencheng Han,Xiao-Hui Li,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Benchmark Agent，一个全自主的智能体系统，能够自动完成从用户查询分析到数据标注和质量控制的完整基准构建流程，生成高质量基准样本，并发现当前模型在特定领域推理任务上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准构建劳动密集、难以复用、可持续性和可扩展性差，且现有基准发布后快速达到性能饱和，无法有效区分最先进模型之间的差异。

Method: 提出Benchmark Agent全自主智能体系统，自动化完成基准构建全流程：用户查询分析、子任务设计、数据标注和质量控制。通过15个代表性基准的构建进行验证，涵盖文本理解、多模态理解和领域特定推理。

Result: 人工评估、LLM-as-a-judge评估和一致性检验表明，Benchmark Agent能够在最小人工参与下生成高质量基准样本；持续评估发现当前模型在特定领域推理任务上表现不足。

Conclusion: 快速演进的基准对研究社区有重要贡献，该系统有望提升基准构建的可持续性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Benchmarks are fundamental for evaluating and advancing LLMs and MLLMs by providing standardized and explicit measures of performance. However, their construction is labor-intensive and hard to reuse, raising concerns about sustainability and scalability. Moreover, existing benchmarks often quickly reach performance saturation after their release, resulting in insufficient discrimination among state-of-the-art models. To address these challenges, we introduce Benchmark Agent, a fully autonomous agentic system designed for benchmark building. Our framework orchestrates the complete benchmark construction pipeline, from user query analysis and subtask design to data annotation and quality control. To assess Benchmark Agent, we implement it to produce 15 representative benchmarks, spanning diverse evaluation scenarios, including text understanding, multimodal understanding, and domain-specific reasoning. Extensive experiments, including human evaluation, LLM-as-a-judge assessment, and consistency checks, demonstrate Benchmark Agent can generate high-quality benchmark samples with minimal human involvement. More importantly, through continual evaluation, we observe several insightful findings, including that current models struggle with certain domain-specific reasoning tasks. We believe that rapidly evolving benchmarks can contribute significantly to the research community. The preview and code will be publicly available at the demo page and code repository.

</details>


### [335] [Goedel-Architect: Streamlining Formal Theorem Proving with Blueprint Generation and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06468)
*Jui-Hui Chung,Ziyang Cai,Zihao Li,Qishuo Yin,Rohit Agarwal,Simon Park,Rodrigo Porto,Narutatsu Ri,Ziran Yang,Shange Tang,Xingyu Dang,Hongzhou Lin,Mengdi Wang,Danqi Chen,Chi Jin,Liam H Fowl,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Goedel-Architect 是一个基于蓝图生成与精化的 Lean 4 形式化定理证明智能体框架。先生成依赖图（蓝图），再并行证明每个开放引理，失败引理驱动全局蓝图精化。以 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 为骨干，在 MiniF2F-test 上达到 99.2% pass@1，PutnamBench 上 75.6% pass@1；配合自然语言种子蓝图，MiniF2F-test 达到 100%，PutnamBench 提升至 88.8%，并在 IMO 2025、Putnam 2025、USAMO 2026 上取得优异成绩，性能达到开源 SOTA，成本低至同类管线的 1/500。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化定理证明方法多采用递归引理分解，容易在死胡同策略上低效循环。本文旨在设计一种全局蓝图引导、可并行证明且能自动精化的智能体框架，以提升证明成功率和效率，同时大幅降低成本。

Method: Goedel-Architect 包含两个核心组件：(1) 蓝图生成器，生成由形式化定义、引理及其依赖关系构成的依赖图，可接受自然语言证明作为可选引导；(2) 装备工具的 Lean 证明器，并行关闭蓝图中每个开放引理节点，利用相关依赖进行证明；失败的引理驱动全局蓝图的精化迭代。

Result: 使用 DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B-A13B) 作为骨干模型：(1) MiniF2F-test 上 99.2% pass@1；(2) PutnamBench 上 75.6% pass@1；(3) 加入自然语言种子蓝图后：MiniF2F-test 达到 100%，PutnamBench 提升至 88.8%（597/672），IMO 2025 解决 4/6，Putnam 2025 解决 11/12，USAMO 2026 解决 3/6；(4) 成本仅为同类开源管线的 1/500。

Conclusion: Goedel-Architect 通过蓝图生成与精化策略，有效避免了递归分解的低效循环问题，在多个数学竞赛基准上达到了开源 SOTA 性能，且成本极低，证明了全局蓝图引导+并行证明+失败驱动精化的范式的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce Goedel-Architect, an agentic framework for formal theorem proving in Lean 4 centered on blueprint generation and refinement. A blueprint is a dependency graph of definitions and lemmas that builds up to the main theorem. First, Goedel-Architect generates a blueprint of formally stated definitions and lemmas, along with declared dependencies. This blueprint is optionally guided by a natural language proof. Then, a tool-equipped Lean prover component closes each open lemma node in parallel using relevant dependencies. Failed lemmas in turn drive refinement of the global blueprint. This strategy contrasts with other mainstream approaches which use recursive lemma decomposition, and can inefficiently loop on dead-end strategies. Using the open-weight DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B-A13B) as the backbone, Goedel-Architect attains 99.2% pass@1 on MiniF2F-test and 75.6% pass@1 on PutnamBench. With an optional natural-language proof seeding the initial blueprint on the harder problems, we additionally close the remaining two MiniF2F-test problems (reaching 100%), lift PutnamBench to 88.8% (597/672), and solve 4/6 on IMO 2025, 11/12 on Putnam 2025, and 3/6 on USAMO 2026. This represents state-of-the-art performance for an open-source pipeline at a price point up to 500x less than comparable open-source pipelines.

</details>


### [336] [MLEvolve: A Self-Evolving Framework for Automated Machine Learning Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06473)
*Shangheng Du,Xiangchao Yan,Jinxin Shi,Zongsheng Cao,Shiyang Feng,Zichen Liang,Boyuan Sun,Tianshuo Peng,Yifan Zhou,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MLEvolve提出了一种基于LLM的自进化多智能体框架，通过渐进式MCGS、回顾性记忆和自适应编码模式，解决了长期机器学习算法发现任务中信息隔离、无记忆搜索和缺乏层次控制的问题，在MLE-Bench上实现了SOTA性能，并在数学算法优化任务上超越AlphaEvolve。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLE智能体存在三个关键缺陷：分支间信息隔离、无记忆的盲目搜索、缺乏层次化的控制机制，这些问题严重阻碍了长期自进化能力的发展。

Method: 1) 渐进式MCGS：扩展树搜索，通过基于图的参考边实现跨分支信息流动，并结合熵启发的渐进调度策略，使搜索从广泛探索逐步转向聚焦利用。2) 回顾性记忆：结合冷启动领域知识库和动态全局记忆，用于任务特定经验的检索与复用。3) 自适应编码模式：将战略规划与代码生成解耦，支持稳定的长期迭代。

Result: 在MLE-Bench上，MLEvolve在平均奖牌率和有效提交率等多个维度上达到SOTA，且仅需12小时预算（标准运行时间的一半）。在数学算法优化任务上超越AlphaEvolve等专用算法发现方法，展现出强跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: MLEvolve通过跨分支信息共享、经验积累的显式记忆机制以及规划与执行解耦的层次化控制，显著提升了LLM智能体在长期自进化任务中的效果和效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to long-horizon tasks such as scientific discovery and machine learning engineering (MLE), where sustained self-evolution becomes a key capability. However, existing MLE agents suffer from inter-branch information isolation, memoryless search, and lack of hierarchical control, which together hinder long-horizon optimization. We present MLEvolve, an LLM-based self-evolving multi-agent framework for end-to-end machine learning algorithm discovery. By extending tree search to Progressive MCGS, MLEvolve enables cross-branch information flow through graph-based reference edges and gradually shifts the search from broad exploration to focused exploitation with an entropy-inspired progressive schedule. To allow the agent to evolve with accumulated experience, we introduce Retrospective Memory, which combines a cold-start domain knowledge base with a dynamic global memory for task-specific experience retrieval and reuse. For stable long-horizon iteration, we further decouple strategic planning from code generation with adaptive coding modes. Evaluation on MLE-Bench shows that MLEvolve achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple dimensions including average medal rate and valid submission rate under a 12-hour budget (half the standard runtime). Moreover, MLEvolve also outperforms specialized algorithm discovery methods including AlphaEvolve on mathematical algorithm optimization tasks, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/InternScience/MLEvolve.

</details>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [337] [Gradient descent at the Edge of Stability: free energy model and kinetic description of the two-layer network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05326)
*Antonin Chodron de Courcel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对梯度下降在“稳定性边缘”（Edge of Stability）阶段的动力学行为进行研究，提出了一种连续时间有效模型，通过平均轨迹与快速振荡的时均协方差耦合来刻画损失与锐度的持续振荡，并引入有效自由能（包含原始风险函数和曲率相关的“熵”项）作为关键监控量。该模型能追踪振荡包络，甚至在权重平均动力学时间尺度相近时也有效。针对宽两层神经网络在稳定非衰减振荡下的训练，推导出描述权重及其波动联合分布的均值场极限，并证明该方程等价于宏观自由能的Wasserstein-2梯度流。矩阵分解和CIFAR-10深度学习的数值实验验证了模型在捕捉振荡包络和有效自由能预测能力方面的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解梯度下降在较大学习率下（Edge of Stability regime）出现的持续振荡现象，揭示其背后的有效动力学规律，并建立能够捕捉振荡包络和宏观行为（如宽神经网络均值场极限）的理论框架。

Method: 提出连续时间有效模型，将平均轨迹演化和快速振荡的时均协方差耦合；引入有效自由能（原始风险+曲率相关熵项）；对宽两层神经网络推导均值场极限，得到新动力学方程并证明其为Wasserstein-2梯度流。

Result: 有效模型能够精确追踪振荡包络，即使在平均权重与振荡动力学时间尺度相近时也适用；均值场极限导出的动力学方程可解释为自由能的Wasserstein-2梯度流；矩阵分解和CIFAR-10实验验证了模型准确性。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解大学习率下梯度下降的非单调不稳定动力学提供了理论工具，有效自由能是监控此类不稳定训练过程的关键量，且框架可推广至宽神经网络等实际架构。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of gradient descent in the Edge of Stability regime, where the learning rate is large enough to induce persistent oscillations in the loss and the sharpness. We propose a continuous-time effective model that tracks the evolution of the average trajectory coupled with the time-averaged covariance of its fast oscillations. Our analysis reveals that the natural quantity to monitor in such unstable regimes is an effective free energy, which combines the original risk functional with a curvature-related "entropic" term. Our model allows us to track the envelope of the oscillations even in situations where its dynamics evolve on similar timescales as the averaged weights. Otherwise stated, we can track the spikes that occur during the training of some neural network architectures. For wide two-layer neural networks optimized under stable non-vanishing oscillations, we derive a mean-field limit that results in a novel kinetic equation describing the joint distribution of weights and their fluctuations. We show that this equation can be interpreted as a Wasserstein-2 gradient flow of a macroscopic free energy. Finally, we provide numerical evidence on matrix factorization and deep learning tasks (CIFAR-10) to demonstrate the model's accuracy in capturing the envelope of the oscillations and the predictive power of the effective free energy.

</details>


### [338] [Learning Implicit Feasibility Constraints for Real-World Routing and Scheduling: Application to Log Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05353)
*Abdelhakim Abdellaoui,Ayoub Boufous,Issmail El Hallaoui,Loubna Benabbou,Francois Aubé,Mouloud Amazouz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的约束学习方法，通过组合GNN、决策树和线性回归三个预测器，并采用堆叠机制嵌入MILP中，解决物流路径规划中因运营规则动态变化导致的手动调整难题。在真实木材运输数据上，该方法在保证可行性的同时，目标退化最小，且无需预先知道规则性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际车辆路径与调度问题中的运营规则频繁变化（季节性/永久性），而现有优化工具内置固定硬编码约束，更新需要建模和运筹学技能，导致规划方案需人工凭经验调整，效率低下。

Method: 基于数据驱动的约束学习方法：在历史执行数据上训练三种互补预测器（图神经网络GNN、决策树、线性回归），通过线性化约束将各预测器嵌入MILP中；进一步引入堆叠机制，将三者组合成单一增强优化问题（AOP），由求解器内生地选择每个决策最可靠的预测器。

Result: 在真实工业木材运输数据上：单一预测器均可提升可行性；堆叠嵌入一致地实现最低目标退化——在未见实例上满足运营规则且退化最小，无需先验知识即可为每个决策选择最合适的预测器，同时减少日常手动调整工作量并保持可解性。

Conclusion: 该框架使优化工具能够适应不断变化的实践，避免重复的手动重新建模，为动态运营规则下的路径优化提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world vehicle routing and scheduling problems involve complex operational rules and feasibility constraints typically formulated as mixed-integer linear programs (MILP). However, optimization tools are built around a fixed set of hard-coded constraints, while in practice this set evolves as new rules or preferences emerge, seasonally or permanently. Updating it requires modeling and operations research skills that planners rarely have, so generated plans are routinely adjusted by hand based on practical knowledge. Building on recent work that uses machine learning to recover such hidden constraints, we propose a data-driven constraint-learning approach that trains three complementary predictors, a Graph Neural Network (GNN), a decision tree, and a linear regression, on historical execution data from a log-truck routing and scheduling problem ($\mathcal{LTRSP}$), and embeds each inside a MILP through linearized constraints. We further introduce a stacking mechanism that combines all three within a single augmented optimization problem (AOP), letting the solver endogenously select the most reliable predictor for each decision. On real-world industrial data, each predictor already improves feasibility, but the stacked embedding consistently achieves the lowest objective degradation: it (i)~satisfies the operational rules on unseen instances with smaller degradation than any single-model variant, (ii)~picks the most appropriate predictor per decision without prior knowledge of the rule's nature, and (iii)~reduces daily manual adjustment effort while remaining tractable for daily use. Beyond this application, the framework enables optimization tools that adapt to evolving practice without recurrent manual remodeling.

</details>


### [339] [Bayesian Equilibria of Heterogeneous Non-Atomic Routing Games with Private Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05424)
*Alexia Ambrogio,Leonardo Cianfanelli,Giacomo Como,Paolo Frasca*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究共享交通网络中协调车队与自私用户两类交通流的非原子贝叶斯路由博弈，证明了在有限状态和信息集下贝叶斯均衡的存在性与唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实交通网络中存在协调车队（如自动驾驶网约车）和自私用户（普通驾驶员）两类参与者，且路况受随机环境影响，双方基于异质不完全信息做出路由决策，亟需理论模型分析此类博弈的均衡性质。

Method: 构建非原子贝叶斯路由博弈模型，假设世界状态和私有信息集有限，链路旅行时间依赖于聚合流量和随机环境状态，两类参与者基于先验分布和私有信号进行路由决策，采用博弈论分析方法证明均衡存在性与唯一性。

Result: 在有限状态和私有信息集的假设下，证明了该贝叶斯路由博弈存在唯一的贝叶斯均衡。

Conclusion: 该模型为分析混合交通流（协调车队与自私用户）在异质不完全信息环境下的路由行为提供了理论基础，均衡的唯一性为后续效率分析和机制设计奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study non-atomic Bayesian routing games whereby a transportation network is shared by two types of traffic: a coordinated fleet and a mass of selfish users. The links in the network are characterized by travel time functions that depend both on the aggregate flow on the link and on a random state of the world $W$ that, in general, is not directly observable. Rather, we assume that both the fleet coordinator and the selfish users know the prior distribution of $W$, observe (different) private messages that are correlated with $W$ and possibly among themselves, and make routing decisions based on such heterogeneous partial information. Under the assumption that both the state of the world and the private message sets are finite, we prove the existence and uniqueness of a Bayesian equilibrium for the ensuing Bayesian routing game.

</details>


### [340] [Optimal experimental design for passive imaging source problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05490)
*Christian Aarset,Thorsten Hohage,Georg Stadler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对被动成像中的最优实验设计问题，提出了一种基于贝叶斯反问题框架和两级低秩近似的A最优设计方法，有效解决了相关数据导致的计算瓶颈，并实现了无需额外PDE求解的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动成像中，相关数据的使用导致观测维度随传感器数量呈平方增长，传统最优设计方法因需要反复求解偏微分方程而面临严重的计算瓶颈。

Method: 采用贝叶斯反问题框架处理被动成像源问题，聚焦于Helmholtz方程控制的空间不相关源。开发了两级低秩近似策略逼近A最优设计目标，将问题解耦为离线阶段和在线阶段，实现设计目标及其梯度的高效评估，无需额外的PDE求解。

Result: 所提算法能够高效扩展到大规模问题，且得到的最优设计在最小化后验不确定性方面显著优于随机传感器布置。

Conclusion: 提出的两级低秩近似方法有效克服了被动成像OED的计算瓶颈，为大规模传感器优化布置提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This work focuses on optimal experimental design (OED) methods for passive imaging. We adopt a Bayesian inverse problem framework for passive imaging source problems, primarily focusing on spatially uncorrelated sources and systems governed by the Helmholtz equation. A major challenge in passive imaging is that the use of correlation data causes the observation dimension to grow quadratically with the number of sensor locations, compounding the computational difficulty of finding optimal designs. To overcome the computational bottleneck of repeated PDE solves in optimal design algorithms, we develop a two-level, low-rank approximation of the A-optimal design objective. This effectively decouples the problem into an offline and an online phase, enabling efficient evaluation of the design objective and its gradient without additional PDE solves. Our numerical results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm efficiently scales to large problems and that the resulting optimal designs significantly outperform random sensor placements in minimizing posterior uncertainty.

</details>


### [341] [On Parallel and Batch-Cutting Strategies for Norm-Minimization-Based Convex Vector Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05617)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对凸向量优化的范数最小化外逼近算法，提出并行化子问题计算和批量切割策略。并行化将8核加速比提升至1.1-4.2倍；批量切割减少62-80%的迭代次数，但加速效果取决于子问题计算成本是否占主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准范数最小化外逼近算法每次迭代需求解N_k个独立子问题，但仅采用单个最优切割，计算资源利用率低，效率有待提升。

Method: 1) 并行化：将子问题评估分配到n_w个工作节点，降低每次迭代的墙钟时间。2) 批量切割：利用所有已求解子问题的信息，每次迭代添加最多K个支撑半空间，而非仅使用最优切割。

Result: 8核并行化实现1.1-4.2倍加速；批量切割减少62-80%的迭代次数。批量切割的墙钟收益依赖于问题特性：当每顶点子问题成本占主导时效果最佳。

Conclusion: 批量切割变体继承了标准算法O(k^{2/(1-q)})的收敛率（k为外迭代次数，q为目标数）。并行化与批量切割相结合可有效提升算法效率，但收益具有问题依赖性。

Abstract: We develop parallel and batch-cutting variants of the norm-minimization-based outer approximation algorithm for convex vector optimization. The standard algorithm solves $N_k$ independent subproblems at each iteration~$k$ to evaluate all vertices of the current polyhedral approximation, but processes only the single best cut. We propose two improvements. First, we parallelize the \revise{subproblem evaluations} across $\nw$ workers, reducing per-iteration wall-clock time. Second, we introduce a batch-cutting strategy that adds up to $K$ supporting halfspaces per iteration, using information from all solved subproblems rather than discarding it. We prove that the batch-cutting variant inherits the convergence rate $O(k^{2/(1-q)})$ of the standard algorithm, where $k$ is the number of outer iterations and $q$ is the number of objectives. Computational experiments on eight test problems with $q \in \{2,3,4,5\}$ show that parallelism on 8 cores \revise{increases the speed by a factor of 1.1 to 4.2}, and batch cutting consistently reduces the iteration count by 62--80\%. However, the wall-clock benefit of batch cutting is problem-dependent: the additional cuts per iteration accelerate vertex count growth, so batch cutting is most effective when per-vertex subproblem cost dominates.

</details>


### [342] [Convergence rate of the Halpern iterations with possibly distinct anchor and initial guess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05630)
*Jianing He,Qiao-Li Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了锚点与初始猜测不同的广义Halpern迭代的收敛速率，给出了预定义和自适应锚定参数下的紧致收敛速率估计，推广了现有工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Halpern迭代的收敛速率估计仅针对锚点与初始猜测相同的情形，而实际应用中两者可能不同，因此需要研究更一般的设定。

Method: 分析了预定义锚定参数和自适应锚定参数两种策略下广义Halpern迭代的收敛行为，推导了相应的紧致收敛速率估计。

Result: 在锚点与初始猜测不同的情况下，给出了预定义和自适应锚定参数下收敛速率的紧致上界，且这些结果推广了已有的相关结论。

Conclusion: 本文的收敛速率估计是紧致的，且统一了锚点与初始猜测相同与不同两种情形，为Halpern迭代的实际应用提供了理论保证。

Abstract: All existing convergence rate estimates of Halpern iterations are established only for the case where the anchor coincides with the initial guess. This paper aims to investigate the convergence rate of general Halpern iterations, where the anchor and the initial guess may not necessarily be the same. We present tight convergence rate estimates for both predetermined and adaptive anchoring parameters. These results generalize existing related work.

</details>


### [343] [Sparse Recovery via $\ell_1^2-η\ell_2^2$ Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05674)
*Lang Yu,Nan-jing Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了加权范数平方差（WDSN）罚项 $\ell_1^2-η\ell_2^2$ 在压缩感知中的精确恢复条件与RIP分析，建立了基于零空间性质（NSP）的充分条件和基于 $δ_{2k}$-RIP的稳定恢复保证，设计了基于近端算子的ADMM算法，并实验证明优于迭代半阈值方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WDSN罚项 $\ell_1^2-η\ell_2^2$ 在压缩感知和逆问题中具有强大的稀疏促进能力和优越的重构性能，但尚未建立其精确恢复保证和RIP分析，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 首先基于零空间性质（NSP）建立k-稀疏信号精确恢复的充分条件；然后在 $δ_{2k}$-RIP条件下推导k-稀疏信号和一般信号的稳定恢复保证及重构误差上界；进一步提出基于WDSN的正则化模型统一处理无噪声和有噪声观测；推导WDSN泛函近端算子的显式公式，并基于此开发ADMM变量分裂方案，证明其全局收敛性。

Result: 建立了WDSN最小化的NSP精确恢复条件和RIP稳定恢复保证，给出了重构误差上界；提出了收敛的ADMM算法；数值实验表明该方法在无噪声和有噪声稀疏恢复任务中均优于迭代半阈值方法。

Conclusion: 本文首次为WDSN罚项提供了完整的理论恢复保证（NSP条件和RIP分析），并设计了高效可收敛的算法，在稀疏恢复中表现出优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: The weighted difference of squared norms (WDSN) penalty $\ell_1^2-η\ell_2^2$ with $0\leq η\leq 1$ has attracted considerable attention due to its strong sparsity-promoting ability and favorable reconstruction performance in compressed sensing and inverse problems. However, exact recovery guarantees and restricted isometry property (RIP) analysis for WDSN minimization have not yet been established. In this paper, we address this gap. First, we establish sufficient conditions for the exact recovery of $k$-sparse signals based on the null space property (NSP). Then, under the $δ_{2k}$-RIP condition, we derive stable recovery guarantees for both $k$-sparse signals and general signals, and characterize upper bounds on the reconstruction error. Furthermore, we propose a WDSN-based regularized model to handle both noiseless and noisy observations in a unified framework. To design an efficient algorithm, we derive an explicit formula for the proximal operator of the WDSN functional. Based on this proximal solver, we develop a suitable variable-splitting scheme within the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) and establish its global convergence under some mild conditions. Finally, numerical experiments show that the proposed method outperforms the iterative half variation method in both noiseless and noisy sparse recovery tasks.

</details>


### [344] [Combining diffuse and sharp interface methods in shape optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05963)
*Philip J. Herbert,Michael Hinze,Christian Kahle*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合相场法和尖锐界面法的两阶段形状优化数值方法：先用相场法确定优化域的拓扑与形状，再用尖锐界面法精化生成角点等尖锐特征，并通过零水平集构建有限元网格衔接两阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场法擅长处理形状优化的拓扑变化但难以生成尖锐边界（如角点），而尖锐界面法擅长精化边界但需要良好初值，因此将两者结合以取长补短。

Method: 两阶段流程：第一阶段用相场法获得优化形状，第二阶段以相场解的零水平集经后处理构建有限元网格，作为尖锐界面形状优化方法的初始解，两种方法均采用有限元离散。

Result: 在多个文献和实际测试问题上验证了方法的性能，展示了从相场初解到尖锐界面精化的完整流程效果。

Conclusion: 结合相场法与尖锐界面法的两阶段策略能有效克服各自局限，实现既能处理拓扑变化又能生成尖锐边界的形状优化。

Abstract: We develop a concept for the numerical treatment of shape optimization problems based on the combination of phase field and sharp interface methods. On the one hand, phase field methods are very well suited to numerically determine the shape, size and topology of a sought domain, but on the other hand they have problems to sharpen out domains where they e.g. should develop corners. However, this is the strength of a sharp-interface approach developed in our group, which provides shape updates in the Lipschitz topology. This leads to a two-stage process that first determines an optimized shape using the phase field method. The resulting domain is the starting solution for the sharp interface shape optimization method. Both methods are discretized with the finite element method. The starting mesh for the sharp method is constructed from the finite element mesh of the optimal phase field solution using its properly post processed zero-level set. We describe this construction process in detail and investigate the performance of our method on a selection of test problems from the literature and from applications.

</details>


### [345] [Distributionally robust shortfall risk portfolio model with moment ambiguity sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05996)
*Yi Yang,Liu Yang,Suhan Zhong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于矩的分布鲁棒优化（DRO）短差风险投资组合模型，假设股票收益与影响因素呈多项式关系。对于分段线性损失函数，该模型可转化为带非负多项式锥的可处理线性锥优化问题，并设计了Moment-SOS松弛算法求解，证明了有限及渐近收敛性，给出了全局最优性验证条件，实证表明方法有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统投资组合风险度量方法（如方差、在险价值等）存在局限性，短差风险能更好刻画尾部风险，但现有模型对收益分布的假设过于严格。本文旨在利用分布鲁棒优化思想，在仅知收益与影响因素的多项式关系及部分矩信息的条件下，构建更稳健的短差风险投资组合模型。

Method: 1) 采用短差风险（shortfall risk）度量组合风险；2) 假设股票收益与影响因素服从多项式关系；3) 构建基于矩的分布鲁棒优化（DRO）短差风险模型；4) 针对分段线性损失函数，证明模型可转化为带非负多项式锥的线性锥优化问题；5) 提出Moment-SOS松弛算法求解转化后问题，并研究有限与渐近收敛性；6) 给出验证求解器全局最优性的便利条件。

Result: 理论方面：证明了DRO模型到可处理线性锥优化问题的转化，以及Moment-SOS算法的有限与渐近收敛性，给出了全局最优性验证条件。实证方面：基于真实股票市场数据的数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于矩的DRO短差风险模型及Moment-SOS求解算法，能够在收益分布信息不完全的情况下有效进行投资组合优化，具有理论上的收敛保证和实际中的计算可行性。

Abstract: This paper employs shortfall risk to measure portfolio risk. Assume stock returns follow polynomial relations with their influence factors. We propose a moment-based distributionally robust optimization (DRO) shortfall risk portfolio model. For piecewise linear loss functions, we show that this DRO model can be transformed into a tractable linear conic optimization problem with nonnegative polynomial cones. A Moment-SOS relaxation algorithm is proposed to solve the transformed problem. Its finite and asymptotic convergence properties are studied. For optimizers computed from our algorithm, we give convenient conditions verify their global optimality for the original DRO problem. Numerical experiments involving real stock market data are given to show the efficiency of our approach.

</details>


### [346] [Uniqueness and stability of Lagrange multipliers and associated qualification conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06067)
*Patrick Mehlitz,Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了抽象空间中约束优化问题拉格朗日乘子的唯一性与稳定性，证明严格Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz条件蕴含拉格朗日乘子映射的孤立 calmness 性质，后者保证乘子唯一性，并在附加假设下与严格RZK条件等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨约束优化问题中拉格朗日乘子的唯一性和稳定性，这是优化理论与灵敏度分析中的核心问题。

Method: 通过分析严格Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz条件与孤立 calmness 性质之间的蕴含关系，并结合反例说明假设的必要性，建立乘子映射的稳定性理论。

Result: 证明了严格RZK条件足以保证拉格朗日乘子映射的孤立 calmness 性质，该性质蕴含乘子唯一性；在附加假设下，孤立 calmness 与严格RZK条件等价；并给出了说明假设必要性的示例。

Conclusion: 孤立 calmness 是分析拉格朗日乘子稳定性和唯一性的有力工具，严格RZK条件在其中起关键作用，但等价性需要额外假设支撑。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with uniqueness and stability of Lagrange multipliers for constrained optimization problems in abstract spaces. It is well known that validity of the strict Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz condition implies the so-called isolated calmness, a one-sided Lipschitz property tailored for set-valued mappings, of some Lagrange multiplier mapping associated with a perturbed version of the original optimization problem, and the latter indeed is enough to guarantee uniqueness of the Lagrange multiplier. The paper studies the isolated calmness of the Lagrange multiplier mapping in detail. Exemplary, it is shown that this condition is sufficient for the Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz constraint qualification and, in the presence of additional assumptions, even equivalent to the strict Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz condition. Illustrative examples are presented to underline the necessity of postulated assumptions.

</details>


### [347] [Second order splitting dynamics for stochastic monotone inclusions with closed loop distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06280)
*Wutao Si,Hamza Ennaji,Jalal Fadili*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Hilbert空间中极大单调算子与余强制算子之和的零点问题，提出基于分布评估的前向-后向分裂算子的连续时间二阶动力学系统，证明了在一致单调性假设下轨迹强收敛至平衡点并具有快速渐近收敛率，以及在强单调条件下具有全局指数收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题自然捕捉了具有决策依赖分布的随机优化问题（即执行预测），旨在解决此类问题的零点求解与收敛性分析。

Method: 提出并分析由分布评估的前向-后向分裂算子驱动的连续时间二阶动力学系统，采用消失粘性阻尼系数（一致单调情形）和常数Polyak型阻尼系数（强单调情形）。

Result: 在一致单调性假设下，证明了轨迹强收敛至平衡点，并获得了速度的快速渐近收敛率；在强单调条件下，建立了全局指数收敛率。

Conclusion: 所提二阶动力学系统能有效求解此类算子零点问题，且在两种阻尼设置下均具有理论保证的收敛性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the problem of finding a zero of the sum of a maximal monotone operator $A$ and a cocoercive operator $\Bm$ in a Hilbert space. This formulation naturally captures stochastic optimization problems with decision-dependent distributions, often referred to as performative prediction. We propose and analyze continuous-time second-order dynamics governed by a distributionally evaluated forward-backward splitting operator. We establish the existence and uniqueness of the equilibrium point under a general uniform monotonicity assumption. In this setting, employing a vanishing viscous damping coefficient, we prove the strong convergence of the trajectories to the equilibrium, accompanied by fast asymptotic convergence rates for the velocities. Furthermore, when the regularizing operator is strongly monotone, we consider a constant Polyak-type damping coefficient and we establish global exponential convergence rates for the dynamical system.

</details>
